  ### Exact expressions

  - **R**, bus basis, per pack, in mOhm:
    - `f0 = curve ( roundToU16 ( rest_mV / cells ) )`
    - `f1 = constrainf ( f0 - used_mAh / C, 0, 1 )`
    - `drop = ( lipoCellRestVoltage ( f0 ) - lipoCellRestVoltage ( f1 ) ) *
  cells`
    - `R = ( rest_mV - drop - load_mV ) * 1000 / ( load_mA - rest_mA )`, then
  `roundToU16 ( constrainf ( R, 80, 250 ) )`.
    - It is not computed if C = 0 or `load_mA <= rest_mA`.
  - **Rest point:** `vBat_mV`, `mAmpRaw` and `mAhDrawn` at every disarmed update
  where neither reading is stale. The last one before arming is used.
  - **Load window:**
    - Loaded time is the sum of the real `dtUs` over updates that are armed, not
  stale, and have `mAmpRaw >= 1500`.
    - `vBat_mV` and `mAmpRaw` are summed over loaded time 25-35 s.
    - `used = mAhDrawn` at the first update with loaded time >= 30 s (the
  midpoint), minus the rest `mAhDrawn`.
    - `load_mV` and `load_mA` are the means over the window.
    - A disarm resets the window. Once R is known it is not measured again.
  - **Vcomp:** `raw = ( vBat_mV + mAmpRaw * R * 0.001 ) / cells`.
    - EMA: `v += dt / ( 1e6 + dt ) * ( raw - v )`, with dt in µs. So alpha = dt
  / ( tau + dt ), about 0.0206 at 21 ms. The first value after R is known seeds
  it.
    - `vBatComp = roundToU16 ( v * cells )` when R is known, else `roundToU16 (
  vBat_mV + mAmpRaw * 0.1 )`.
  - **Pull-down:**
    - `countRemain = E > drawn ? E - drawn : 0`
    - `reported = countRemain - mAhPullDown` (saturating at 0).
    - If `vCompValid` and `frac = curve ( v ) <= 0.25` and `voltRemain = floor (
  frac * C ) < reported`, then `mAhPullDown = countRemain - voltRemain` and
  `reported = voltRemain`.
    - A final guard `reported = min ( reported, reportPrev )` means it never
  rises.
    - `vCompValid` means R is known, the EMA has a value, and the voltage is not
  stale.
  - **SoC:** `soc_Fused = C > 0 ? constrainf ( reported * 100 / C, 0, 100 ) :
  0`, and `mAhRemain = reported`. Both are held (0 at boot) while no battery is
  present or the plug-in estimate is not ready.
  - **Conditions:**
    - Voltage warning: R known and `v <= 3745.0`; voltage critical: `v <=
  3600.0`.
    - Count warning: `countRemain * 100 <= C * 15`; count critical: `<= C * 5`.
  The count test uses `countRemain`, not the pulled-down value, as specified,
  and only when `batteryCountAvailable ( )`.
  - **Debounce:** `heldUs = cond ? addSat ( heldUs, dtUs ) : 0`; the state steps
  down when `heldUs >= 1500000`. Both timers are zeroed while not ready.
  - **Clear (disarmed only):**
    - `warnClear = !warnCount && ( !R known || ( vCompValid && v > 3795 ) )`
    - `critClear = !critCount && ( !R known || ( vCompValid && v > 3650 ) )`

  ### State machine as implemented

  | From | Condition | To | Actions |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | OK (every update) | none | none | `ENABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( OK_TO_ARM )`,
  `BatteryWarningMode = 0` |
  | OK | ready and warn debounced and `fsInFlightLowBattery` | WARNING |
  `set_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_LOW )`, mode 1 |
  | OK | same, and crit debounced too | CRITICAL (same update) | the WARNING
  actions above, then `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`, `reset_FSI (
  Low_battery )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | WARNING (every update) | none | none | `DISABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( PREVENT_ARMING
  )` |
  | WARNING | crit debounced | CRITICAL | `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`,
  `reset_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | WARNING | disarmed and `warnClear` | OK | `reset_FSI ( Low_battery )`, mode
  0 |
  | WARNING | otherwise | stays | `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_LOW )`, mode 1 |
  | CRITICAL (every update) | none | none | `DISABLE_ARMING_FLAG (
  PREVENT_ARMING )` |
  | CRITICAL | disarmed and `critClear` | WARNING | `set_FSI ( Low_battery )`,
  `reset_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_LOW )`, mode 1 |
  | CRITICAL | otherwise | stays | `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | NOT_PRESENT | none | none | nothing |

  A critical condition always implies the warning condition (3600 < 3745 and 5%
  < 15%), so the warning timer is never behind the critical one. From OK the
  state therefore goes through WARNING in the same update, with both steps'
  flags. It never jumps to CRITICAL with only the second step's flags.

  ### Stale handling

  - **Bus stale** (12 failed calls in a row, 252 ms):
    - `vBatComp` and the EMA are held, and the pull-down is skipped.
    - The voltage conditions keep their last value, so the debounce carries on
  as if the condition were still there. They are never cleared: the clear test
  needs `vCompValid`.
    - The R window pauses.
  - **Shunt stale:**
    - The last good current is still integrated while armed, but not while
  disarmed.
    - The R window pauses (it is not aborted), and no rest point is taken.
    - Vcomp keeps using the held `mAmpRaw`.
  - **Settle cap:** if 24 good samples are not in after 96 calls since connect,
  the estimate is made from the samples collected (if at least 1). With none, E
  = 0 and the estimate is marked ready. Count remaining is then 0, so the
  warning and critical states follow 1.5 s later.
  - `batterySensorStale`: bit 0 = voltage, bit 1 = current.

  ### Readers of what changed meaning or was removed

  - `soc_Fused` is now remaining / capacity. Read by MSP_ANALOG
  (`serial_msp.cpp:1001`), CRSF (`mw.cpp:456`) and `PlutoPilot.cpp` `S`.
  - `mAhRemain` is now the reported (pulled-down, held) remaining. Read by MSP
  (`serial_msp.cpp:1000`) and `BMS.cpp:39` `Bms_Get`.
  - `vBatComp` is now bus + I x R. Read by MSP (`serial_msp.cpp:997`) and
  `PlutoPilot.cpp` `Vc`.
  - `BatteryWarningMode` is now 2 in CRITICAL. Read by MSP
  (`serial_msp.cpp:1002`).
  - `getBatteryState ( )` has new trigger rules. Read by `ledstrip.c:654` and
  `hott.c:224`.
  - `batteryWarningVoltage` / `batteryCriticalVoltage` are still computed but
  trigger nothing. `failsafe.cpp:103` only declares an extern.
  - Removed with no remaining readers: `soc_battery_percentage`,
  `soc_mAh_percentage`, `wAh`, `vShuntRaw`.
  - `mAhDrawn`, `EstBatteryCapacity` and `vBatRaw` are unchanged.

  ### Deviations from the design

  1. **R subtracts the rest current:** `( rest - drop - load ) / ( I_load -
  I_rest )` instead of `( OCV1 - V_load ) / I_load`. The rest reading is taken
  at about 125 mA, so it is about 15-20 mV below the true OCV. The design form
  would read R about 4-5 mOhm low; the difference form cancels that error
  exactly and uses the rest current you asked me to keep. Example: rest 4150 mV
  at 125 mA, load 3750 mV at 4000 mA, 35 of 600 mAh used gives R = 92 mOhm.
  2. **R and all model state reset on a battery connect** (`batteryModelReset`),
  not only at power-off. Normally connect is the power-up. On USB power a
  swapped pack would otherwise inherit the old pack's R and pull-down.
  3. **The count test needs the count to exist:** `INA219_Current` defined and
  `FEATURE_INA219_CBAT` on. PRIMUSX2 has no plug-in estimate (E is always 0), so
  it would have warned at once.
  4. Used mAh is taken at the midpoint, as specified: `mAhDrawn` at 30 s loaded
  minus the rest value.
  5. `BatteryWarningMode` is also set on transitions, not only while staying in
  a state.
  6. `vBatComp` (display) is computed even with no battery present, as before.
  Only the model steps need a present battery.

  ### Unsure / for the reviewer

  - **PRIMUSX2 (legacy):** SoC and `mAhRemain` read 0 there (E = 0). The only
  warnings are voltage ones after R is measured, if its INA219 shunt works. Not
  built.
  - **`FEATURE_INA219_CBAT` switched off at runtime:** no R and no count, so no
  warnings at all. Before, the voltage SoC still warned. The feature is on by
  default on every target.
  - **Clamp of a bad high R:** it clamps to 250 mOhm. Clamping high puts Vcomp
  high, which is the late-warning side. A negative or garbage low R clamps to 80
  (the safe side). The design asked for a plain clamp.
  - **`ENABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( OK_TO_ARM )` timing:** it now runs every 21 ms
  instead of every loop. `mw.cpp:476` also sets it every loop, so the effect
  should be nil.
  - **Not done yet:** the task 5 done-when offline replay of the log-6
  `V`/`I`/`D` data through the new rule. Nothing has been flown yet, and the
  `pluto-reviewer` pass is still to run.

● Task 5, the charge model and low-battery warnings, is built and passes the
  build gate. It stays open until the mandatory reviewer pass comes back.

  What changed:
  - Removed: the old blended SoC, which followed a straight voltage line and
    warned with about 3% left.
  - Resistance: measured once per power-up, from the rest voltage before arming
    and 25-35 s of flight drawing at least 1.5 A.
  - Warning: the compensated voltage (V + I × R) at or below 3.745 V per cell,
    or 15% of capacity left by the count, whichever comes first.
  - Critical: at or below 3.60 V, or 5% left.
  - Debounce and latch: a condition must hold for 1.5 s, and a warning stays on
    until landing.
  - Remaining mAh: follows the count; the voltage can pull it down only once it
    says 25% or less is left, and the figure never rises.
  - Dead sensor: readings count as stale after about 250 ms without a good read.
    The battery update now runs every 21 ms instead of every loop.

  Build: no new warnings and 13 old ones in battery.cpp gone. Flash is 101.2 KB
  (+1.1 KB); RAM 14.8 KB.

  Replay of the new rule over your two full-to-empty flights:

  ┌─────────────────┬───────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────┐
  │                 │         Newer 600         │         Older 600         │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ Resistance      │ 151 mΩ                    │ 168 mΩ                    │
  │ measured        │                           │                           │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ Warning         │ 16.5% left, 58 s before   │ 17.7% left, 40 s before   │
  │                 │ landing, at 3.10 V        │ landing, at 3.04 V        │
  ├─────────────────┼───────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────┤
  │ App remaining   │ 29 mAh, about 24 real     │ 23 mAh, about 49 real     │
  │ at landing      │ (today's count alone      │ (reads low, which is the  │
  │                 │ would show 60)            │ safe side)                │
  └─────────────────┴───────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────┘

  On the newer pack critical came 2 s after landing, because you touched down
  about 1 s after the critical condition started. On the older pack you landed
  at 9%, before critical.

  I've asked the reviewer to check one risk in particular. A pack flown straight
  off the charger reads high at rest, which makes the measured resistance too

  ## Review: battery-soc-fix task 5 (4 files, 7 findings)

  I checked each finding against the code. I also replayed the rule as built on
  topic logs log-1 and log-2 (scratchpad script). The replay reproduces R = 151
  / 169 mOhm and the warning at about 17% and 13% true charge left. Both logs
  are single hover flights that end empty, so what they can show is limited.

  1. [BLOCKING] battery.cpp:674-679, 859, 892-897 - If R is never measured, the
  voltage path stays off for the whole power-up. The count is then the only
  trigger, and it does not fire when the configured capacity is larger than the
  real pack.
     Rule: general (flight safety); task 5 "Done when: an empty pack cannot read
  high"
     Failure:
     - `rLoadedUs` and the window are reset on every disarm (675). R therefore
  needs 35 s of ≥1.5 A load inside one arming. A power-up made of hops shorter
  than that never measures R.
     - Example: ELRS setup forces `BatteryCapacity = 800` (RxConfig.cpp:172) and
  a 600 pack is fitted. E = 800, so the count warns only at 680 mAh drawn and
  goes critical at 760. The pack holds about 567. Result: no warning before the
  pack is empty, and `soc_Fused` still reads about 29% at empty.
     - An aged pack (real capacity below rated) gives the same result.
     - INVESTIGATION §5.7 accepts "count carries" until R is known, but §5.6
  says the floor "uses a low R" until then. The two sections disagree, and the
  Done-when is not met.
     Fix: keep the R window running across armings within the power-up (rest
  point from before the first arming, charge used to the midpoint), and/or add a
  voltage backstop while R is unknown. This needs a user decision.

  2. [BLOCKING] battery.cpp:588-594, 693 with mw.cpp:432-441 - With
  `FEATURE_INA219_CBAT` off there is no low-battery warning at all. This is a
  regression: the old code fell back to the voltage SoC.
     Rule: general (regression)
     Failure:
     - `updateINA219Current` never runs, so `mAmpRaw` stays 0. R never passes
  the 1500 mA gate, `countOn` is false, and no condition can become true.
     - `soc_Fused` stays frozen at E / C for the whole flight.
     - PRIMUSX2 (no `INA219_Current`, CBAT set at config.cpp:414): the count is
  always off and E = 0, so `mAhRemain` and `soc_Fused` read 0 permanently. Only
  the R-gated voltage path is left, which is issue 1 with no count behind it.
     Fix: when `! batteryCountAvailable ( )`, fall back to a voltage-only rule,
  or state explicitly that these configurations are unsupported.

  3. [SHOULD-FIX] battery.cpp:869-907, 750 - The voltage pull-down and the
  voltage warning conditions also run while disarmed. Right after a landing,
  `Vcomp` reads low because the pack has not relaxed yet.
     Rule: general (false warning; wrong remaining figure)
     Failure:
     - In the logs, the EMA `Vcomp` falls 34-78 mV below the last armed value
  after disarm (log-1 3610 to 3532, below by more than 50 mV for about 9 s;
  log-2 3630 to 3596).
     - Take a mid-pack landing at `Vcomp` of about 3.80 V (40% on the curve). A
  dip to about 3.72-3.75 V holds for more than 1.5 s. That trips WARNING on the
  ground (Low_battery FSI and beeper) and permanently pulls `mAhRemain` down to
  about 18-24%.
     - If the pilot re-arms before `Vcomp` passes 3795 mV, the warning stays
  latched for the whole next flight.
     - In-flight wander has the same effect: log-1 305-330 s drifts 3829 to 3774
  to 3829 mV at a steady 4.3 A. Just above the 25% point, that locks in an
  early pull-down.
     - Caveat: the dip was only measured at empty. The mid-pack size needs a
  mid-pack landing log.
     Fix: run the pull-down and the voltage conditions only while armed with
  `mAmpRaw` ≥ 1500, or only after a settle time at rest current.

  4. [SHOULD-FIX / note] battery.cpp:680-685, 626-629 - A pack plugged in
  straight off the charger has surface charge. The rest reading of 4.20 V or
  more gives f0 = 1.0 and a curve drop of only about 58 mV, so R reads high.
     Rule: general (late side)
     Failure: an extra 30-50 mV at rest gives about +10 mOhm, which puts `Vcomp`
  about 40-45 mV high in hover. The warning then fires at a true 3.70 V (about
  12.5% left instead of 17%) and critical at about 3.56 V (about 4%). The count
  still covers this only if the configured capacity is right.
     Fix: accept and document, or take the rest point only after the idle
  average has been flat for a few seconds.

  5. [note] battery.cpp:844-859 - `vCompValid` ignores `BATTERY_STALE_CURRENT`.
     Rule: general
     Failure:
     - If the shunt read goes stale during a high-current moment, `Vcomp` uses
  the frozen high current and reads high, which delays the voltage warning.
     - If the shunt is dead from boot while the bus read is fine: `mAmpRaw`
  stays 0, the count never moves, and R is never measured. No warning at all.
     Fix: decide what a stale current should do to `Vcomp`. One option is to use
  the lower of the frozen and the rest current.

  6. [note] battery.cpp:786, 905 - A critical clears on the ground once `Vcomp`
  passes 3650 mV.
     Failure:
     - On log-2 the empty pack (about 4% true) recovers from 3630 to above 3650
  mV about 10 s after landing. A pack that went critical would therefore step
  down to WARNING while still empty.
     - A re-arm starts in WARNING, and critical only returns after the EMA plus
  the 1.5 s debounce.
     - Also, `mwDisarm` clears `LowBattery_inFlight` (mw.cpp:565) while the
  state stays CRITICAL. That is older behaviour, so the app loses the FSI flag
  after landing while `BatteryWarningMode` stays 2.
     Fix: design choice; consider a larger clear margin for critical.

  7. [note] PIPELINE_UPDATE.md:24 still says "`vShuntRaw` survives until task
  5"; it has now been removed. CHANGES.md task 4 still mentions
  `[[maybe_unused]]`, which the code no longer has.

  Answers to your specific checks:
  - **E = 0 at the settle cap:** the count warns at once and the critical never
  clears until reboot. This is on the safe side.
  - **`fsInFlightLowBattery` false:** nothing fires, not even critical. Same as
  before.
  - **Punch or EMA seed:** the only false-early paths found are issue 3's
  disarmed and relaxation dips. When R is in, the EMA is seeded from a 1
  s-averaged raw value, which is fine.
  - **Re-armed right after a landing (R still unknown):** R reads low, which
  errs toward an early warning.
  - **R clamp:** at 250 mOhm, hover `Vcomp` rises by about 1 V, which disables
  the voltage path. Only a bad rest point or a genuinely weak pack gets there.
  - **Units:** mV pack vs per cell, mA x mOhm / 1000, us and the uint16
  saturation are all consistent. The inverse curve is correct at the table
  points (5% gives 3610 mV, 0% gives 3270 mV).

● The reviewer found two blocking issues and five others. Three of the fixes
  change how the warning behaves, so I need you to decide those.

  Blocking:
  1. Resistance not yet measured. Measuring it takes 35 s of steady load in one
     arming, so a session of short hops never gets it. Until then only the mAh
     count can warn. With the capacity set higher than the pack really holds,
     the count alone never warns before empty. For example, ELRS setups default
     the capacity to 800 mAh: a 600 pack would reach empty with the app still

  6. Critical on the ground: it clears once an empty pack recovers a little,

● I've sent the fix list to cpp-pro and fixed the two stale doc lines. The
  fixes:

  1. Resistance not yet measured: use a default 100 mΩ, and let the 35 s
     measurement add up across short hops.
  2. No current sensor: fall back to the raw voltage against your stored 3.2 V /
     3.0 V cell settings.
  3. Latch: warnings stay on until power-off, and the voltage checks run only in
     flight under load.
  4. Off the charger: the rest reading is capped at 4.18 V per cell for the
     resistance measurement.
  5. Sensor dropout: when the current reading goes stale, the voltage check uses
     zero current, which errs toward warning early.

  `batteryResistanceUpdate` in detail:
  - It returns at once when R is measured or when `rLoadedUs >= 35 s`: one
  attempt per power-up.
  - While disarmed, the rest point is taken only while `! rArmedOnce && !
  stale`, with `rest_mV = min ( vBat_mV, 4180 x cells )` and a comment on
  surface charge.
  - While armed it sets `rArmedOnce`. It does not reset on disarm: loaded time,
  the V and I sums, and the midpoint `mAhDrawn` all carry across hops.
  - Stale readings or `mAmpRaw < 1500` pause the window.

  ### New transition table (latched; each state runs every update)

  | From | Condition | To | Actions |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | OK | every update | stays | `ENABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( OK_TO_ARM )`,
  `BatteryWarningMode = 0` |
  | OK | ready and ( warnLow or critLow ) and `fsInFlightLowBattery` | WARNING |
  `set_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_LOW )`, mode 1 |
  | OK | same, and critLow | CRITICAL (same update) | the WARNING actions, then
  `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`, `reset_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `beeper (
  BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | WARNING | critLow | CRITICAL | `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`, `reset_FSI
  ( Low_battery )`, `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | WARNING | otherwise | stays | `DISABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( PREVENT_ARMING )`,
  `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_LOW )`, mode 1 |
  | CRITICAL | always | stays | `DISABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( PREVENT_ARMING )`,
  `beeper ( BEEPER_BAT_CRIT_LOW )`, mode 2 |
  | NOT_PRESENT | none | none | nothing |

  - **No upward transitions.** Only `handleBatteryConnected ( )` sets OK again:
  a battery connect, which is normally a power-up. A disconnect sets NOT_PRESENT
  as before.
  - **Debounce:** `warnLow = warnVoltUs >= 1.5 s || warnCountUs >= 1.5 s`, and
  the same for `critLow`.
  - **Why OK accepts `warnLow || critLow`:** in voltage-only mode a config with
  `vBatMinVoltage` above `vBatWarningVoltage` could make critical fire without
  warning. It still goes through WARNING first.

  ### Exact expressions

  **Default R and Vcomp (count mode and `vBatComp` in both modes):**
  - `r_mOhm = batteryResistance_mOhm != 0 ? batteryResistance_mOhm : 100`.
  - `i_mA = currStale ? 0 : mAmpRaw`.
  - `raw = ( vBat_mV + i_mA * r_mOhm * 0.001 ) / cells`.
  - EMA: `v += dt / ( 1e6 + dt ) * ( raw - v )`, dt in µs; `vBatComp =
  roundToU16 ( v * cells )`. Both are held while the bus is stale.
  - `vCompValid = vCompCellInit && ! voltStale`, so it no longer needs R to be
  measured.

  **Count mode** (`batteryCountAvailable ( )`: `INA219_Current` defined and
  `FEATURE_INA219_CBAT` on):
  - `loaded = armed && mAmpRaw >= 1500`.
  - The pull-down only runs when `loaded && vCompValid`; otherwise the formula
  is as before.
  - Voltage timers:
    - When not loaded, both are 0.
    - When loaded and `vCompValid`, `debounceStep ( t, v <= 3745 )` and
  `debounceStep ( t, v <= 3600 )`.
    - When loaded and the bus is stale, they hold.
  - Count timers always run: `countRemain * 100 <= C * 15` and `<= C * 5`.

  **Voltage-only fallback** (`! batteryCountAvailable ( )`):
  - `rawCell_mV = vBat_mV / cells` (uint32).
  - When the bus is not stale:
    - `soc = lipoCellRestFraction ( rawCell_mV ) * 100`.
    - If `armed && socPrevInit && soc > socPrev`, then `soc = socPrev`.
    - `soc_Fused = soc`.
    - `mAhRemain = ( uint16_t ) ( soc * 0.01 * C )`, floored, so 0 when C is 0.
  - When disarmed, the voltage timers are 0.
  - When armed and the bus is not stale:
    - `warnVoltUs = debounceStep ( warnVoltUs, rawCell_mV <= vBatWarningVoltage
  * 100 )`.
    - `critVoltUs = debounceStep ( critVoltUs, rawCell_mV <= vBatMinVoltage *
  100 )`.
  - When armed and the bus is stale, the timers hold. The count timers are
  always 0.
  - The same actions, latching, `batteryEstimateReady` gate and
  `fsInFlightLowBattery` gate apply.
  - R is still measured and logged in this mode, but no decision uses it.

  **Unchanged:** before the plug-in estimate is ready (or with no battery), all
  four timers are 0, `soc_Fused` and `mAhRemain` are held, and
  `updateBatteryState ( false, false )` runs.

  ### Unsure / for the reviewer

  1. **No R for the whole power-up:** this happens if there was no valid rest
  point at the first arming (the readings were stale on every disarmed update
  before it). The 100 mOhm default is used and I did not add a later retry,
  because a retry would have to use a post-landing snapshot, which item 1 rules
  out. The same applies if the completed window gives an R that cannot be
  computed.
  2. **Stale current in count mode:** `loaded` uses the frozen `mAmpRaw`. So
  with the current stale, loaded flight means the last good current was at least
  1500 mA. Vcomp uses I = 0 (item 5), so it reads low, the early side.
  3. **Pull-down on a default-R Vcomp:** with R still unknown, the pull-down now
  works from takeoff on a Vcomp computed with 100 mOhm. On a 150 mOhm pack at 4
  A, Vcomp reads about 200 mV per cell low. If the resting-curve fraction of
  that value is at or below 25%, `mAhRemain` / `soc_Fused` are pulled down
  early, and the pull-down is permanent for the power-up. On a mid-charge pack
  this happens when the true Vcomp is below about 3.95 V (a 3.75 V reading is
  the 25% point). The user asked for the default in "the voltage conditions and
  the pull-down", so this is per the decision, but the SoC shown in the first 35
  s of loaded flight can be low for good. A replay would show how large the
  effect is.
  4. **Voltage-only mode:** the config thresholds are compared inclusively
  (`<=`). Their defaults were given as 3.2 / 3.0 V; I did not check the stored
  defaults. SoC may rise while disarmed (it follows the resting voltage) and
  never rises while armed. PRIMUSX2 and PRIMUS_V5 were not built.
  5. **Not done yet:** the offline replay of the task 5 done-when, and a
  reviewer re-pass on this revision.

  - E:\08-DRONA-AVIATION\99-Dump\MagisV2\src\main\sensors\battery.h

● One point in the agent's notes (its item 3) needs correcting before the
  replay.

  When you chose the default 100 mΩ, I said it could only warn early during the
  first ~35 s. Combined with "stay on until power-off", that's no longer true. A
  half-used pack after a power-up reads about 3.62 V compensated in hover with
  the default. The warning would then fire within 1.5 s and stay on for the
  whole flight, and the remaining mAh would be pulled down to about 5% for good.

  To make it behave as I described, a warning raised by the default resistance
  will be provisional. It can clear once the real resistance is measured and the
  condition no longer holds, and the default never pulls the remaining down.
  Sending that to the agent:

  ### Exact provisional logic

  **Setting the flag** (end of `BMS_Update`, after `updateBatteryState`):
  ```
  if ( batteryState != prevState ) {    // always a step down
    countCaused      = ( batteryState == BATTERY_CRITICAL ) ? ( critCountUs >=
  1.5 s ) : ( warnCountUs >= 1.5 s );
    alarmProvisional = countMode && ! rKnown && ! countCaused;
  }
  ```
  - A step taken with R measured, or caused by the count condition of the level
  stepped to, sets the flag false: the alarm is real.
  - The voltage-only fallback never sets it (`countMode` false), which covers C.

  **Resolving the flag** (count mode, on the update R is measured):
  ```
  if ( rJustMeasured && alarmProvisional ) {
    alarmProvisional = false;
    voltNow = vCompValid && vCompCell_mV <= 3745;    // EMA already re-seeded
  with the measured R
    if ( ! voltNow && ! warnCountNow ) {
      batteryState = BATTERY_OK;
      reset_FSI ( Low_battery );
      reset_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight );
      BatteryWarningMode = 0;
      warnVoltUs = 0;
      critVoltUs = 0;
    }
  }
  ```
  - This runs before the state machine in the same update. The OK case then runs
  straight away and re-enables `OK_TO_ARM`. The cleared timers keep it from
  stepping down again at once, and `warnCountNow` is false, so the count timer
  is 0 too.
  - R is only measured in a loaded update with fresh readings (stale readings
  pause the R window), so `vCompValid` is true there and the check runs in
  loaded flight.
  - If R is never measured in the power-up, the provisional alarm stays (the
  early side).

  ### Notes / unsure

  - **"Hold" uses the warning-level test** (Vcomp <= 3745 or count <= 15%),
  because a critical condition implies the warning one. Consequence: a
  provisional CRITICAL whose measured-R Vcomp sits between 3600 and 3745 mV
  stays CRITICAL; it does not drop to WARNING. That follows the brief ("keep the
  state"). Say if it should step to the supported level instead.
  - **Mixed-cause steps:** a WARNING that was real (count-caused) followed by a
  CRITICAL step caused only by the default-R voltage marks the CRITICAL
  provisional. At the R-known re-check the count warning condition still holds,
  so it stays CRITICAL as a real alarm. The reverse case: a provisional WARNING
  followed by a count-caused CRITICAL becomes real.
  - **Flash +320 B** is more than the logic suggests. It is probably inlining or
  register pressure in `BMS_Update` under `-Os`. I did not investigate (the nm
  tool was not on PATH in my shell).
  - **Not done yet:** the offline replay of the task 5 done-when, and a reviewer
  re-pass on this revision.

  ## Re-review: battery-soc-fix task 5 after the review fixes (battery.cpp,
  battery.h; 6 findings)

  **Status of the 7 earlier findings.** I read the code and replayed the new
  rules on logs/log-1.txt and log-2.txt (scratchpad script).
  1. Resolved: until R is measured the voltage check uses the default 100 mOhm,
  and the R window now adds up across armings.
  2. Resolved: there is a voltage-only fallback when current sensing is missing.
  New number issues: finding 3 below.
  3. Resolved: the voltage conditions and the pull-down run only in loaded
  flight (lines 896 and 919-925), so the dip after landing can no longer trip
  them.
  4. Resolved: the rest point is capped at 4180 mV per cell (line 711). What is
  left of surface charge is about +3-7 mOhm, a small late error.
  5. Resolved: a stale current is treated as I = 0 (line 869), which errs early.
  See the note in finding 6.
  6. Resolved, because nothing clears on the ground any more. The latch brings a
  new FSI problem: finding 2.
  7. Partly resolved: PIPELINE_UPDATE.md line 24 and CHANGES.md line 48 are
  fixed. New stale text: finding 5.

  ## Findings

  1. [SHOULD-FIX, high] battery.cpp:864, 919-925, 983-986 - The default R of 100
  mOhm raises a warning early in almost every flight on a pack that is not
  full. If the pilot lands before 35 s of loaded time, that warning stays
  latched until power-off.
     Rule: general (false latched warning)
     Failure:
     - With the default R, Vcomp = OCV - I x (R_true - 100). The measured packs
  are 146-169 mOhm, so in hover Vcomp reads about 200-300 mV low.
     - Replay at the default R, hover values: the warning condition holds from
  **72.7% (log-1) / 79.8% (log-2) true left**, and critical from **46.7% /
  45.6%**.
     - So any pack at about 70-80% or less when plugged in gets a provisional
  warning in its first ~35 s of flight. A pack at about 45% or less gets a
  provisional critical.
     - The only way back up is the update where R gets measured (line 933), and
  that needs 35 s of loaded time in total.
     - A pilot who lands when the alarm sounds, which is the right response,
  never reaches 35 s. The alarm then stays until power-off, and after a
  power-cycle it fires again.
     - The result is a latched false alarm on healthy packs (alarm fatigue).
     Fix: needs a user decision. Options:
     - Allow the default-R voltage alarm only while the count says the pack is
  low, e.g. count remaining ≤ 35-40% of C. That still catches the 800-on-567
  case, which reads 29% at empty.
     - Use a default R near the measured median (~140 mOhm) instead of 100.
     - Also re-check provisional alarms at the next arming, not only on the
  update R is measured.

  2. [SHOULD-FIX] mw.cpp:565 with battery.cpp:805-809 - Once CRITICAL is
  latched, the `LowBattery_inFlight` FSI flag is lost at the first disarm and
  never set again.
     Rule: general; review item 4 (flags consistent per transition)
     Failure:
     - `mwDisarm ( )` calls `reset_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`.
     - The CRITICAL state never calls `set_FSI` again, and `Low_battery` was
  already reset on the step to CRITICAL.
     - So when the pilot re-arms the same pack, which is now latched critical
  until power-off, no battery FSI flag is set. The app's
  `App_LowBattery_inFlight` bit (serial_msp.cpp:890), the status LED
  (runtime_config.cpp:258) and RGB-LED.cpp:328 all show nothing.
     - Only the beeper and `BatteryWarningMode` = 2 remain.
     - The mwDisarm behaviour is older, but before this change the ground clear
  re-set `Low_battery`. Under a latch it is permanent.
     Fix: in `case BATTERY_CRITICAL` call `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )` on
  every update (and `set_FSI ( Low_battery )` in WARNING).

  3. [SHOULD-FIX] battery.cpp:945-972 - With the stored 3.2 / 3.0 V the fallback
  warns at about half charge, and the SoC it shows in flight is about 0-5% from
  takeoff.
     Rule: general (units are right, the scale is wrong)
     Failure:
     - Units are consistent: the config is per cell in 0.1 V, times 100 gives
  mV, compared with `vBat_mV / cells`.
     - But the fallback compares the **loaded** bus voltage with values meant
  for rest. Replay: the warning (bus ≤ 3200 mV held 1.5 s) latches at **53.6% /
  59.0% true left**, and critical (≤ 3000 mV) at 5.3% / 12.9%.
     - A sustained climb on a mid pack (hover bus about 3.23 V at 60%, plus
  about 2 A x 150 mOhm of extra sag) can hold ≤ 3.0 V for 1.5 s and latch
  CRITICAL at around 60%. This is my estimate; the logs are hover only.
     - `soc_Fused` is the rest curve read with the loaded voltage (line 951). In
  hover that is 3.0-3.6 V, which the curve reads as 0-5.6%. Because it cannot
  rise while armed, the app shows ≤ 5% for the whole flight, and `mAhRemain`
  follows it.
     - The investigation's own numbers put the loaded warning point at about
  3.0-3.1 V and critical at 2.9-2.95 V.
     Fix: confirm with the user. Options: add a fixed sag allowance, use the
  hover-measured thresholds, or hold `soc_Fused` at its disarmed value while
  armed. On PRIMUSX2 the current is measured (CBAT is set by config.cpp:414), so
  it could compensate there.

  4. [SHOULD-FIX] battery.cpp:933-944 - Resolving a provisional **CRITICAL**
  checks only the warning-level condition, so the alarm never drops to WARNING.
     Rule: general
     Failure:
     - A pack at about 20-25% is plugged in. The default R gives Vcomp about
  3.53 V, and a provisional CRITICAL fires.
     - When R is measured, Vcomp with the measured R is about 3.72-3.74 V: ≤
  3745 (warning) but > 3600 (critical). `voltNow` is true, so the state stays
  CRITICAL, latched until power-off, with about 20% left.
     - The same happens when the warning was count-caused and only the step to
  critical came from the default R: `warnCountNow` keeps the CRITICAL.
     Fix: on resolution, set the level the measured-R voltage and count
  conditions support: OK, WARNING (`set_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `reset_FSI (
  LowBattery_inFlight )`, mode 1) or CRITICAL.

  5. [note] Docs are out of sync:
     - PIPELINE_UPDATE.md:37-38 still says "latched while armed" and "the stored
  warning / min cell voltages do not trigger". Lines 41-43 below it say the
  opposite.
     - The CHANGES.md:71 row still describes the ground clear with +50 mV.
     - battery.cpp:115-116 say the stored voltages trigger nothing. The fallback
  now uses `batteryConfig->vBat*Voltage`.

  6. [note] Smaller items that stay:
     - When R is measured without a provisional alarm, the EMA is not re-seeded
  (line 871). A voltage timer already running can be completed by one or two
  default-R-weighted updates. Negligible, but re-seeding and resetting the
  voltage timers whenever `rJustMeasured` is cleaner.
     - A stale current with I = 0 latches CRITICAL on a full pack if the stale
  read lasts about 2.5 s or more (EMA 1 s plus 1.5 s debounce). This is the
  user's early-side choice.
     - Capacity C = 0, or E = 0 from the settle cap, gives a count-caused
  CRITICAL at once, even disarmed, latched until power-off. Safe side.

  **Still able to leave an empty pack with no warning:**
  - `fsInFlightLowBattery` false (unchanged, by design).
  - `FEATURE_INA219_VBAT` off (unchanged).
  - A bus reading that is stale in flight while the voltage condition was false:
  the voltage timers are held at 0. Count mode still warns from the count; the
  fallback has nothing. Needs a sensor fault.
  - A measured R near the 250 clamp: the voltage path is late, and only the
  count backs it.
  - I found no single-fault path on the default configuration.

  **Checked and clean:**
  - Ordering: the provisional resolution runs before `updateBatteryState` in the
  same update, and the voltage timers are reset. No re-step in the same update.
  - The provisional clear resets both FSI flags and sets `BatteryWarningMode` 0.
  The beeper sequences end on their own (`BEEPER_COMMAND_STOP`).
  - `alarmProvisional` is set only on a state change and reset on connect. There
  is no path back up besides connect and the provisional clear.
  - R window across hops: the rest point is frozen at the first arming. It
  pauses on stale readings and on current below 1.5 A. The midpoint charge used
  is counted from the rest point. `rSamples` and the sums are bounded. One
  attempt per power-up.
  - Fallback: `batteryEstimateReady` is set on every target (outside the
  `#ifdef`), and it respects `fsInFlightLowBattery`. With C = 0, `mAhRemain` is
  0 and there is no division.
  - Review items 6 (mw.cpp and PlutoPilot.cpp unchanged), 8, 10, 11, 12, 13, 17,
  18.
  - Gate: `tools/warnings.py check Build/PRIMUS_X2_v1/build.log` (the log is
  newer than battery.cpp) reports no new `src/` warnings.

  **Not checked:**

  ### Exact provisional / re-level logic (count mode)

  **Default-R voltage test window:**
  ```
  loaded     = armed && mAmpRaw >= 1500
  voltActive = loaded && ( rKnown || countRemain * 100 <= C * 40 )
  if ( ! voltActive || rJustMeasured ) { warnVoltUs = 0; critVoltUs = 0; }
  else if ( vCompValid ) step both voltage timers as before    // held while the
  bus is stale
  ```

  **Setting the flag** (after `updateBatteryState`):
  ```
  if ( batteryState != prevState ) {    // steps are always down
    countCaused      = ( batteryState == CRITICAL ) ? critCountUs >= 1.5 s :
  warnCountUs >= 1.5 s;
    alarmProvisional = countMode && ! rKnown && ! countCaused;
  }
  ```

  **Re-level** (before `updateBatteryState`, same update):
  ```
  if ( alarmProvisional ) {
    if ( rJustMeasured ) {    // EMA already re-seeded with the measured R
      warnV = vCompValid && v <= 3745;
      critV = vCompValid && v <= 3600;
      batteryApplyLevel ( batterySupportedLevel ( warnV || warnCountNow, critV
  || critCountNow ) );
      alarmProvisional = false;    // latched unless OK
    } else if ( ! armed ) {
      batteryApplyLevel ( batterySupportedLevel ( warnCountNow, critCountNow )
  );
      alarmProvisional = false;    // always count-supported or OK
    }
  }
  ```
  - `warnCountNow = countRemain * 100 <= C * 15`, `critCountNow = countRemain *
  100 <= C * 5`. These are instantaneous; the count cannot recover, so they are
  safe.
  - `batteryApplyLevel`:
    - CRITICAL: `set_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`, `reset_FSI ( Low_battery )`,
  mode 2.
    - WARNING: `set_FSI ( Low_battery )`, `reset_FSI ( LowBattery_inFlight )`,
  mode 1.
    - OK: `reset_FSI` both, mode 0.
  - In the same update the state machine then runs that level's case: the arming
  flag, the repeated beeper and the FSI re-assert. On the disarm path the
  voltage timers are 0. On the R path they were just zeroed. So a voltage
  condition cannot step the state again at once.
  - Count-caused and measured-R steps latch until power-off or a battery
  connect.

  **Transitions:** the table is the same as round 2, except WARNING and CRITICAL
  now re-assert their FSI flag every update, and the two re-level events above
  are the only way up.

  ### Fallback expressions (no current sensing)

  - `rawCell_mV = vBat_mV / cells`.
  - `socCell_mV = armed ? rawCell_mV + 450 : rawCell_mV`, clamped to 0xFFFF.
  - `soc = lipoCellRestFraction ( socCell_mV ) * 100`; if `armed && socPrevInit
  && soc > socPrev`, then `soc = socPrev`.
  - `soc_Fused = soc`, `mAhRemain = ( uint16_t ) ( soc * 0.01 * C )`.
  - Warning at `rawCell_mV <= vBatWarningVoltage * 100` (default 3000 mV);
  critical at `<= vBatMinVoltage * 100` (default 2900 mV); armed only; 1.5 s
  debounce; latched.
  - At the arming edge the disarmed resting SoC caps the armed value, since the
  SoC never rises while armed.

  ### Readers of `vBatWarningVoltage` / `vBatMinVoltage`

  - `serial_msp.cpp:1129, 1131`: MSP_MISC reply (min, then max, then warning).
  - `serial_msp.cpp:1225-1226`: MSP_VOLTAGE_METER_CONFIG reply.
  - `serial_msp.cpp:1702-1703`: MSP_SET_VOLTAGE_METER_CONFIG writes them (from
  the app).
  - `battery.cpp` `setCellCountAndThresholds`: pack-scaled copies into
  `batteryWarningVoltage` / `batteryCriticalVoltage` (no decisions read them).
  - `battery.cpp` `BMS_Update`: the no-current fallback.
  - `config.cpp:311-312`: defaults.
  - There are no CLI or failsafe readers (`failsafe.cpp:103` only declares
  `extern batteryCriticalVoltage`, unused).

  ### Unsure

  - **New defaults apply only to a fresh EEPROM.** `resetConf` writes them only
  when the stored config is reset (EEPROM version change or a defaults reset). A
  board with a saved config keeps its 32 / 30 until the app writes new values.
  I did not bump `EEPROM_CONF_VERSION` (107); you said not to touch anything
  else in config.
  - **While armed, a provisional alarm is not re-evaluated.** It only re-levels
  on disarm or when R is measured. That follows the brief ("NOT latched" defined
  by those two events).
  - **Mixed cause:** a latched count WARNING plus a CRITICAL step from the
  default-R voltage marks the CRITICAL provisional. On disarm it re-levels to
  the count level (WARNING), and that WARNING stays latched.
  - **Not done yet:** the offline replay of the task 5 done-when, and a reviewer

  ## Round 3 re-review: battery-soc-fix task 5 (battery.cpp, battery.h,
  config.cpp; 4 findings)

  **Status of the round 2 findings.** I read the code and replayed the
  no-current fallback on logs/log-1.txt and log-2.txt (scratchpad script).
  - **1 (default-R false alarm latched): resolved.**
    - The default-R check runs only while loaded and with the count at 40% of C
  or less (line 970).
    - A provisional alarm is re-levelled to the count's level on the first
  disarmed update (lines 989-992).
    - When R is measured, it is re-levelled to the measured-R voltage or the
  count (lines 983-988).
    - A pilot who lands on a provisional alarm is back at the count level at
  once.
  - **2 (critical FSI lost after disarm): resolved.** CRITICAL re-asserts
  `LowBattery_inFlight` every update (line 813), so it is back within 21 ms of
  `mwDisarm ( )`. WARNING re-asserts `Low_battery` (line 805).
  - **3 (fallback thresholds / SoC): partly resolved.** The numbers still miss;
  see findings 1 and 2 below.
  - **4 (provisional critical never drops to warning): resolved.**
  `batterySupportedLevel ( )` / `batteryApplyLevel ( )` return CRITICAL, WARNING
  or OK with the matching FSI pair and mode.
  - **6 (EMA re-seed and timers when R is measured): resolved.** The EMA is
  re-seeded on every `rJustMeasured` (line 919) and the voltage timers are
  zeroed (line 971).

  ## Findings

  1. [SHOULD-FIX] battery.cpp:1016-1025, config.cpp:311-312 - The fallback at
  3.0 / 2.9 V warns late, below the 15% target, and its critical can fail to
  fire before landing.
     Rule: general (task 5 Done-when: warning with ≥ 15% left, critical at about
  5%)
     Failure:
     - Replay of the raw loaded bus voltage per cell (1 s average, held 1.5 s,
  armed):

       | Threshold | log-1 fires at | log-2 fires at |
       |---|---|---|
       | 3.0 V (warning) | 5.3% true left | 12.9% true left |
       | 2.9 V (critical) | never (lowest armed reading 2941 mV, landed at 4.8%)
  | 4.4% |

     - A sweep shows the loaded voltage is flat and differs by pack:

       | Threshold | log-1 | log-2 |
       |---|---|---|
       | 3.15 V | 40% | 44% |
       | 3.10 V | 13% | 27% |
       | 3.05 V | 8% | 18% |
       | 2.95 V | never | 8% |

     - Round 2 had 3.2 V warning at about 55%. 3.0 V now warns at about 5% on
  log-1.
     Fix: user decision. 3.10 / 3.00 V gives a warning at 13-27% and critical at
  5-13% on these two packs. Make the pick knowing that no fixed loaded
  threshold hits 15% on both packs.

  2. [SHOULD-FIX] battery.cpp:170, 1002 - In the fallback, the +450 mV hover sag
  is too small for PRIMUS_X2_v1. The app's SoC falls to about 5% by half charge
  and stays there, because it cannot rise while armed.
     Rule: general (scale)
     Failure:
     - Measured hover sag is 4.3 A x 146-169 mOhm, about 630-730 mV per cell.
     - Replay (app SoC against true charge left):

       | Log | ~90% | ~81% | ~74% | ~65% | ~52-56% | below that |
       |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
       | log-1 | 67% | 55% | 30% | 17% | 8% (at 56%) | 4.3% until the end |
       | log-2 | 57% | 30% | 16% (at 71%) | 7.6% (at 62%) | 5.6% (at 52%) |
  ~4-5% |

     - It also drops about 13-40 points at takeoff.
     - Display only (MSP_ANALOG, CRSF, `Bms_Get ( mAh_Remain )`); no warning
  depends on it.
     Fix: about 650 mV for this craft, or hold the disarmed SoC while armed.

  3. [SHOULD-FIX / note] config.cpp:311-312 with serial_msp.cpp:1700-1705 - The
  new defaults will not reach most boards.
     Rule: general
     Failure:
     - `MSP_SET_VOLTAGE_METER_CONFIG` writes max, warning, min and capacity in
  one message. Setting the capacity from the app also writes the app's own
  warning / minimum voltages, which the decisions log gives as 3.2 / 3.0 V. The
  fallback thresholds go back to 3.2 / 3.0 V (warning at about 55%, see round
  2).
     - Boards already configured keep 32 / 30 until a config reset:
  `EEPROM_CONF_VERSION` was not bumped, as you noted.
     - CLI `vbat_*_cell_voltage` (serial_cli.cpp:403-404) point at other fields
  (`vbatmincellvoltage`) and range 10-50, so they neither set nor show these.
     - Only the fallback path (CBAT off, PRIMUSX2) is affected; count mode
  ignores these values.
     Fix: confirm what the app sends. Either use constants for the fallback or
  document that the app's values win.

  4. [note] battery.cpp:985-987 - When R is measured, the re-level uses the
  instantaneous measured-R `Vcomp`: one re-seeded sample from the 1 s averages,
  not debounced. From that point it latches.
     - If R is measured during a descent right after a climb, `Vcomp` can read a
  few tens of mV low and latch WARNING, where a debounced value might not.
     - This only matters when a provisional alarm is already up at that update.
  Low impact.
     - Option: re-level from the count only, and let the measured-R voltage
  timers raise a latched alarm through the normal 1.5 s debounce.

  **Checked and clean:**
  - **Ordering:**
    - The re-level runs before `updateBatteryState ( )`, and `prevState` is
  taken after it (line 1033). The re-level never changes `alarmProvisional`
  through the step check.
    - A step in the same update is judged by `countCaused`. A re-level to OK
  cannot re-step at once: the voltage timers are 0 (disarmed, or
  `rJustMeasured`), and the count is already part of the supported level.
  - **Real alarms cannot be cleared:**
    - `batteryApplyLevel ( )` runs only while `alarmProvisional` is set.
    - When a provisional WARNING later gets a count condition, it re-levels to
  WARNING, not OK.
    - A count WARNING followed by a default-R CRITICAL re-levels to WARNING on
  disarm.
    - After R is measured, no step can be provisional again. Nothing survives a
  disarm as provisional, and a re-arm starts from the count level.
  - **Flags per level:**
    - OK: `ENABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( OK_TO_ARM )`, mode 0, both FSI flags reset.
    - WARNING: `Low_battery` set, `LowBattery_inFlight` reset, mode 1.
    - CRITICAL: the reverse, mode 2.
    - WARNING and CRITICAL still call `DISABLE_ARMING_FLAG ( PREVENT_ARMING )`
  as before. Beeper sequences end on their own after a re-level.
  - **40% gate:** `countRemain x 100 <= C x 40` in uint32. When C = 0 or E = 0
  (settle cap), the gate is open, but the count CRITICAL is already real and
  latched (safe side).
  - **Empty pack with no warning, count mode:** only if R is never measured for
  the whole power-up and the count reads more than 40% at empty. R stays
  unmeasured only with less than 35 s of loaded time in total, or no rest point
  (sensor stale before the first arming), or a stale reading pausing the window.
  Each needs a sensor fault or a flight too short to empty a pack. Unchanged
  residuals: `fsInFlightLowBattery` false, `INA219_VBAT` off, a measured R near
  the 250 clamp (late, the count backs it).
  - **False latched warning on a healthy pack, count mode:** only stale current
  with I = 0 (your choice) and the note in finding 4.
  - Review items 6, 8, 10, 11, 12, 13, 18.
  - Gate: `tools/warnings.py check Build/PRIMUS_X2_v1/build.log` (the log is
  newer than battery.cpp and config.cpp) reports no new `src/` warnings.

  **Not checked:**
  - PRIMUS_V5 and PRIMUSX2 builds (review item 9). PRIMUSX2 is where the
  fallback runs, and its hover sag may differ from PRIMUS_X2_v1.
  - The topic docs, which you are fixing.
  - The fallback replay uses PRIMUS_X2_v1 hover logs only.

● Nothing blocking in round 3, and the main path (with the current sensor) came
  through clean. Everything left concerns the no-current fallback, which only
  runs with current sensing off or on the legacy PRIMUSX2.

  The 3.0 V / 2.9 V I recommended for it was wrong. Those were the loaded
  voltages at which the compensated check fires, but a fixed loaded threshold
  behaves differently because the loaded voltage is flat and varies by pack:
  - 3.0 V warns at only 5% / 13% left on your two packs.
  - 2.9 V critical never fired on one of them.
  - The in-flight SoC drops to about 5% by half charge, because real hover sag
    is 630-730 mV, not the 450 mV I assumed.
  - The app overwrites the stored values: when the app sets the capacity, it
    also writes its own warning and minimum (3.2 / 3.0 V), so new defaults
    wouldn't stick on most boards.

  The reviewer also noted that when resistance gets measured, re-levelling a
  provisional alarm uses one undebounced voltage sample. I'll change that to
  re-level from the count only and let the voltage raise a latched alarm through
  the normal 1.5 s debounce. That needs no decision.

  ### Resulting behaviour

  - **R measured with a provisional alarm:** the state re-levels to the count
  level only (OK / WARNING / CRITICAL, with the matching FSI flags and mode).
  The voltage timers were zeroed on that update and the EMA re-seeded, so if the
  measured-R Vcomp condition holds, a latched alarm follows after 1.5 s of
  debounce.
  - **Fallback:** warning at raw cell <= 3100 mV and critical at <= 3000 mV,
  armed only, 1.5 s, latched. Armed SoC reads the curve at raw cell + 650 mV and
  never rises while armed.

      192  ### 6. `Bms_Get` and `MSP_ANALOG` values against the new model
      193  

● Task 5, the charge model and low-battery warnings, is done. It took four
  review rounds, and the last one found nothing to fix.

  How it works now (PRIMUS_X2_v1, with the current sensor):
  - Remaining mAh follows the charge count, starting from the plug-in estimate.
    Once the voltage says 25% or less is left, it can pull the figure down, and
    the figure never rises.
  - Resistance is measured each power-up over 25-35 s of flight drawing at least
    1.5 A, adding up across short hops. The rest reading is capped at 4.18 V
    per cell so a pack straight off the charger doesn't skew it.
  - Low battery fires when the compensated voltage is at or below 3.745 V per
    cell, or 15% of capacity is left by the count. In hover that's about 3.0-3.1
    V measured.
  - Critical fires at 3.60 V, or 5% left; about 2.9-2.95 V measured in hover.
  - Debounce and latch: a condition must hold for 1.5 s, and a warning stays on
    until the battery is unplugged.
  - Before resistance is measured, the voltage check uses 100 mΩ, but only when
    the count says 40% or less is left. Warnings it raises are provisional: they
    drop back to what the count supports on landing, or when the resistance
    gets measured.
  - Without a current sensor (current sensing off, or the legacy PRIMUSX2):
    fixed 3.10 V warning and 3.00 V critical on the loaded voltage. The app's
    stored cell voltages are no longer used, because the app rewrites them every
    time it sets the capacity.
  - Dead sensor: readings count as stale after about 250 ms without a good read.
    The battery update now runs every 21 ms instead of every loop.

  Replay on your two flights: warning at 16.5% and 17.7% actually left, and
  about 5% shown in the app at empty. Powering up a half-charged pack gives no
  false alarm. With capacity set to 800 on a pack holding about 567 mAh, the
  voltage still warns at 11% left.

  Build: clean on PRIMUS_X2_v1 (13 old warnings in battery.cpp gone); flash
  101.7 KB (+1.5 KB), RAM 14.8 KB. PRIMUS_V5 and PRIMUSX2 are built at commit.
  The fallback thresholds are only validated on PRIMUS_X2_v1 logs, since no
  PRIMUSX2 flight data exists.

  Next is task 6: check what Bms_Get and the app's MSP_ANALOG message now report
  under the new model, and update the API wiki. Bms_Get(Current) now reads
  about 5% higher because the gain is gone, so it also needs a decision on
  bumping API_Version.

  report under the new model. It's mostly a check and docs task:

  - Values whose meaning changed:
    - soc_Fused is now remaining ÷ capacity.
    - mAhRemain can now be pulled down by the voltage near the end.
    - vBatComp is now voltage + current × resistance.
    - BatteryWarningMode is now 0 / 1 / 2 for OK / warning / critical.

● Task 6's code and wiki are done and the build passes. The task stays open
  until the reviewer comes back.

  - Bms_Get changes:
    - Three new options: SoC (0-100%), Warning_Level (0 OK, 1 low, 2 critical)
      and Resistance (mΩ, 0 until measured).
    - Voltage now returns exact mV.
    - Current now returns the current without the old gain, so it reads about 5%
      higher.
    - The new options go at the end of the list, so existing user code keeps
      working.
  - API version: 1.3.2 → 1.4.0 in the Makefile. FW_Version gets bumped at
    commit.
  - New wiki: docs/API/BMS_API_WIKI.md. It covers each option's unit and when it
    becomes valid, how the low-battery level is decided (including the
    provisional exception and the no-current fallback), and what changed from
    1.3.x. Each example sits in its lifecycle hook: print the pack in
    onLoopStart, a live line and "land on critical" in plutoLoop, the resistance
    in onLoopFinish.
  - Build: PRIMUS_X2_v1 builds clean; flash 101.7 KB, RAM 14.8 KB. None of the
    three new names was already in use anywhere in the code.
  - Other readers of the battery values: the app's MSP_ANALOG message keeps its
    10-byte layout. CHANGES.md now lists what each reader gets and in which
    unit, covering MSP, CRSF, FrSky/HoTT/SmartPort, the OLED and the LEDs. CRSF
    current still reads 10 times too high, an older defect left out of scope.

› Message from a9742ef8b6a7d828e
  ## Review: battery-soc-fix task 6, `Bms_Get` API, `API_Version` and BMS wiki
  (4 files, 10 findings)

  There are no blocking findings. The code change is clean. Every should-fix
  item below is a claim in the wiki (and one header comment) that the source
  contradicts.

  1. [SHOULD-FIX] docs/API/BMS_API_WIKI.md:32 and src/main/API/BMS.h:43 - "The
  same figure the app shows" is not exactly true.
     Rule: #15 (wiki accuracy)
     Failure: `Bms_Get ( SoC )` rounds: `static_cast< uint16_t > ( soc_Fused +
  0.5f )` at BMS.cpp:49. `MSP_ANALOG` truncates: `constrain ( soc_Fused, 0, 0x65
  )` at serial_msp.cpp:1001, and `constrain` takes an `int`, so the float is
  truncated. With soc_Fused = 14.6 the app shows 14 and `Bms_Get` returns 15.
     Fix: say "rounded; the app truncates, so it can read 1 lower".
  Alternatively truncate in `Bms_Get`, but the user chose rounding.

  2. [SHOULD-FIX] BMS_API_WIKI.md:42-43 - "and at 3.75 V / 3.60 V with the drone
  at rest" suggests the level can be raised at rest.
     Rule: #15
     Failure: the voltage test runs only in loaded flight: armed and `mAmpRaw >=
  1500` (battery.cpp:949, :975). With the rest thresholds it only ever runs in
  flight (battery.cpp:1018). A pack resting at 3.70 V on the bench raises
  nothing through voltage; only the count can raise it.
     Fix: "compensated (resting-equivalent) 3.745 / 3.60 V per cell, checked in
  flight only".

  3. [SHOULD-FIX] BMS_API_WIKI.md:40-41 and :46-47 - the "whichever comes first"
  rule and the provisional exception are incomplete.
     Rule: #15
     Failure (a): before R is measured, the voltage test is active only while
  the count shows 40% or less left (battery.cpp:975). During the first ~35 s of
  flight above 40%, only the count can trigger.
     Failure (b): the exception says "count still above 15%". But a *critical*
  raised by voltage while the count is between 5% and 15% is also provisional
  (battery.cpp:1039-1040). On disarm, or once R is measured, it drops back to
  low battery (battery.cpp:992).
     Fix: reword. Before R is measured, voltage acts only at 40% or less by
  count. A voltage-only step to a level the count does not support may drop to
  the level the count supports.

  4. [SHOULD-FIX] BMS_API_WIKI.md:29, :31-32 and :54-55 - wrong for boards
  without current sensing.
     Rule: #15
     Failure (PRIMUSX2 has `INA219_Current` commented out at target.h:143; the
  same applies with `FEATURE_INA219_CBAT` off):
     - `Estimated_Capacity` stays 0 for good, because the estimate is inside
  `#ifdef INA219_Current` (battery.cpp:414-428). "Wait for `Estimated_Capacity`
  to be non-zero" therefore never completes.
     - `SoC` and `mAh_Remain` come from the voltage on the curve plus a 650 mV
  sag, not from remaining / capacity (battery.cpp:1003-1014).
     - `mAh_Remain` can rise while disarmed, because the no-rise clamp applies
  only while armed (battery.cpp:1008). That contradicts "Never rises during a
  power-up".
     - In count mode E can also legitimately stay 0: pack at or below 3.27 V at
  rest, or the settle cap with no good read (battery.cpp:505).
     Fix: add a "without current sensing" note covering these three options.
  Make the timing advice "wait ~1 s after power-up" rather than "E non-zero".

  5. [SHOULD-FIX] BMS_API_WIKI.md:110-116 - the "Changes in 1.4.0" table has no
  `mAh_Consumed` row.
     Rule: #15
     Failure: 1.3.x integrated `mAmpWithGain` in ms steps (HEAD
  battery.cpp:348-350). Now the counter integrates the current with no gain, in
  mA x us (battery.cpp:601). `mAh_Consumed` therefore reads ~5% higher in
  flight, and `mAh_Remain` falls about 5% faster.
     Fix: add a row: "no gain, exact dt: ~5% higher than before".

  6. [NOTE] BMS_API_WIKI.md:97-104 - the `onLoopFinish` example does not fit the
  hook convention.
     Rule: example placement convention
     - `onLoopFinish` is for cleanup. A one-time read and print belongs in
  `onLoopStart` (read on the next Developer Mode entry).
     - The comment "0 means ... under ~35 s in the air" is inaccurate. The 35 s
  is loaded time (armed, at least 1.5 A) added up across every flight this
  power-up (battery.cpp:733). The value is also 0 for good without current
  sensing, or when the pack was armed before a rest reading was taken.

  7. [NOTE] BMS_API_WIKI.md:34 and BMS.h:45 - "Pack resistance" is really the
  resistance at the bus.
     - It includes the 20 mOhm shunt and the wiring (battery.h:97) and is
  clamped to 80-250 mOhm (battery.cpp:155-156, :760).
     - It reads ~25-60 mOhm above a charger IR figure (decision log 2026-09-28).
     - Worth one line so users do not compare it with their charger.

  8. [NOTE] BMS_API_WIKI.md:113 - "settled at 0.95" needs a qualifier.
     - The old gain started at 1.0 every boot and learned only while armed above
  its minimum current (HEAD battery.cpp:261-286).
     - Before the first flight of a power-up, the old `Current` already equalled
  the new value. "~5% higher" applies only in and after flight.

  9. [NOTE] BMS.h:42 and :32-34 - two stale comments.
     - The enum comment for `Estimated_Capacity` still says "estimated
  capacity". The doc comment at :61 now says "charge at plug-in".
     - The enum brief does not list the three new options.
     Rule: #18 / style (nit)

  10. [NOTE] docs/fw-development-reference/active-development/battery-soc-fix/CH
  ANGES.md:31, :33 - the task 2/3 rows still say "`Bms_Get` still reads
  `vBatRaw` / `mAmpWithGain`". The task 6 section supersedes them. An "(until
  task 6)" tag would stop them reading as current.

  Checked and clean:
  - **Enum values and ABI:** the new options are appended.
  `Voltage`..`Estimated_Capacity` stay 0-5 and the new ones are 6-8, so nothing
  shifts for user code or `BUILD_TYPE=LIB` libraries.
  - **Name clashes:** a whole-word search for `SoC`, `Warning_Level` and
  `Resistance` across src/, lib/, PlutoPilot.cpp and PlutoPilot.h finds no other
  identifiers or macros.
  - **Conversions (#10, #12):**
    - `soc_Fused` is kept within 0-100 (battery.cpp:968, and the curve maxes at
  100 at :1007), so `+ 0.5f` and the cast give 0-100.
    - Only float math, no double.
    - `uint8_t` `BatteryWarningMode` and the other `uint16_t` values widen to
  the `uint16_t` return with no conversion.
  - **Includes and externs:** battery.h declares `vBat_mV`, `mAmpRaw`,
  `soc_Fused`, `BatteryWarningMode` and `batteryResistance_mOhm` inside `extern
  "C"`. battery.cpp defines them and includes that header, so the linkage
  matches.
  - **Race check (#11):** `BMS_Update` and `Bms_Get` both run from the main
  loop, not an ISR.
  - **Banners (#18):** both banners are kept, with history lines added.
  - **MSP_ANALOG:** serial_msp.cpp:995-1003 is untouched and still 10 bytes.
  - **CRSF:** mw.cpp:456 reads the globals directly, not `Bms_Get`, so task 6
  does not affect it.
  - **Version (#15):** `API_Version` 1.3.2 -> 1.4.0; `FW_Version` is bumped at
  commit.
  - **Wiki examples compile against the real API:**
    - `Monitor_Print` / `Monitor_Println ( const char*, int )` is chosen through
  `uint16_t` -> `int` promotion; the double overloads need 3 arguments
  (Debugging.h:78, :88).
    - `FlightStatus_Check ( FS_ARMED )` matches FC-Control.h:69.
    - `Command_Land ( )` uses the default argument 105 (FC-Control.h:110).
  Calling it again while landing does nothing, because of the guard at
  FC-Control-Command.cpp:139.
  - **Byte budget (#6):** the longest example line is ~45 bytes per tick.
  - **Hook placement:** Quick Start, Live line and Land on critical in
  `plutoLoop`, and the `onLoopStart` example, are all correct.
  - **Wiki numbers confirmed against the source:**
    - averaging 50 x 21 ms ≈ 1 s; estimate 24 x 21 ms ≈ 0.5 s
    - 15% / 5% count thresholds and 1.5 s debounce
    - latch until power-off
    - failsafe enable is on by default (API-Utils.cpp:122)
    - fallback 3.10 / 3.00 V per cell, armed only (battery.cpp:173-174,
  :1018-1024)
    - old estimate was a straight line from 3.0 to 4.2 V with the default config
  (config.cpp:309-310)
    - old `mAh_Remain` could wrap
  - **Dashes:** no non-ASCII bytes in the wiki, BMS.h or BMS.cpp, so no em or en
  dashes.
  - **Other docs that might mention `Bms_Get`:** CLAUDE.md,
  ARCHITECTURE_OVERVIEW.md and Power_BMS_Pipeline.md (describes committed code,
  only names the files; its rewrite is task 10) say nothing about its values.
  There is no docs/API index to update.
  - **PlutoPilot.cpp log field `V`:** still in mV, same digit width.
  - **Checklist items:** #1-5, #7, #9, #13, #16, #17 and #19 do not apply or
  passed.

  Not checked:
  - **#14 warning gate:** no build.log exists, so it was not run. It is task 7.
  - **CRSF current 10x high:** noted as out of scope, not re-reviewed.

      37 -  Voltage,              // The voltage of the battery.         
      38 -  Current,              // The current flowing through the batt
         -ery.                                                           
      38 +  Voltage,              // Battery voltage in mV ( averaged ). 
      39 +  Current,              // Battery current in mA ( averaged ). 
      40    mAh_Consumed,         // The milliampere-hours consumed from 
          the battery.
      41    mAh_Remain,           // The milliampere-hours remaining in t
          he battery.
      42    Battery_Capicity,     // The total capacity of the battery in
           milliampere-hours.
      42 -  Estimated_Capacity,   // The estimated capacity of the batter
         -y in milliampere-hours.                                        
      43 -  SoC,                  // State of charge in percent ( 0 .. 10
         -0 ): remaining / capacity, as the app shows.                   
      43 +  Estimated_Capacity,   // Charge in the pack at plug-in in mil
         +liampere-hours, from its resting voltage.                      
      44 +  SoC,                  // State of charge in percent ( 0 .. 10
         +0 ): remaining / capacity, rounded ( the app truncates ).      
      45    Warning_Level,        // Low-battery level: 0 = OK, 1 = low b
          attery, 2 = critical ( latched until power-off ).
      45 -  Resistance            // Pack resistance measured this power-
         -up in milliohms ( 0 until measured ).                          
      46 +  Resistance            // Resistance at the battery sensor ( p
         +ack, wiring, shunt ) in milliohms, 0 until measured.           
      47  } BMS_Option_e;
      48

● Task 6 is done. The reviewer found no code problems, only accuracy gaps in the
  wiki and a few comments, and I've fixed all of those.

  Wiki fixes (docs/API/BMS_API_WIKI.md):
  - SoC: the app shows the same value truncated, so it can read 1 lower than
    Bms_Get(SoC), which rounds.
  - Voltage check: it runs only in flight, drawing at least 1.5 A. On the ground
    only the charge count can raise the level.
  - Before resistance is measured: the voltage check acts only once the count
    shows 40% or less. The provisional rule now also covers a critical the count
    doesn't support.
  - New "Without current sensing" section: on PRIMUSX2 the start estimate stays
    0 and the SoC follows the voltage, so it can rise on the ground. With
    current sensing off, current, mAh used and resistance also stay 0.
  - Timing: wait about 1 s after power-up before reading SoC or remaining,
    rather than waiting for the estimate to be non-zero (it stays 0 on PRIMUSX2
    or a flat pack).
  - 1.4.0 change table: now includes mAh_Consumed, which reads about 5% higher
    without the gain.
  - Resistance: defined as measured at the sensor, including wiring and the 20
    mΩ shunt. It reads 25-60 mΩ above a charger's IR figure.
  - Resistance example: moved to onLoopStart, per your hook convention.

  I also updated the BMS.h enum comments and marked two older CHANGES.md rows as
  superseded by task 6.

  Next is task 7, the build gate on PRIMUS_X2_v1 over the whole topic diff: no
  new warnings, and flash and RAM reported. After that comes task 8, the bench
  supply sweep, which you run. Say next to start task 7.
